TL;DR: 本文开发了一种上下文感知学习框架，将图像对比度与元数据（成分、束能量、探测器几何）相结合，用于透射电镜中的缺陷分类。通过对96种掺杂单层过渡金属二硫属化物构建约5500万模拟图像块的数据集，该框架在模拟数据上达到98%以上准确率，在实验数据上接近人类水平，后验熵降低94%。该方法将缺陷分类从仅基于图像的病态问题转变为物理上有依据的良态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电子显微镜中基于AI的材料表征大多仅依赖图像对比度，忽略了化学和实验上下文信息，导致缺陷分类存在固有的模糊性——相似的对比度可能来自不同材料或成像条件。

Method: 构建了一个上下文感知学习框架，将图像对比度特征与描述成分、束能量和探测器几何的元数据相结合。使用了约5500万模拟图像块的数据集，涵盖96种掺杂单层TMD材料中的576种情况。

Result: 模拟数据上分类准确率超过98%，实验数据上接近人类专家水平，后验熵降低了94%。

Conclusion: 通过强调上下文信息而非架构复杂性，该框架将实验图像对比度与潜在的化学和成像条件联系起来，为自主材料表征的多模态AI模型提供了一条通用路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is rapidly advancing materials characterization, yet most applications in electron microscopy rely solely on image contrast, overlooking the chemical and experimental context that shapes image formation. This limitation makes defect classification inherently ambiguous, as similar contrasts can arise from different materials or imaging conditions. Here we develop a context-aware learning framework that integrates image-derived contrast with metadata describing composition, beam energy, and detector geometry. Using a systematically constructed dataset of ~55 million simulated patches spanning 576 cases across 96 doped monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides, we show that conditioning on contextual variables transforms defect classification from an ill-posed image-only task into a well-posed, physically grounded problem. The framework achieves over 98% accuracy on simulations and near-human agreement on experimental data, with a 94% reduction in posterior entropy. By emphasizing contextual grounding over architectural complexity, this approach links experimental image contrast to the underlying chemical and imaging conditions, supporting physically grounded defect assignments and a general pathway toward multimodal AI models for autonomous materials characterization.

</details>


### [89] [A Robust Agentic Framework for Expert-Level Automation of Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09422)
*Yutack Park,Yeonwoo Chung,Jinmu You,Jisu Kim,Suyeon Ju,Seungwu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍Paimon，一个用于原子模拟的智能体集成平台，通过自动化输入准备和数据分析等机械流程，显著提升工作流可靠性并抑制静默错误，实现从文献复现到全生命周期模拟的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子模拟受限于从头算方法的计算扩展限制和经验力场的参数化开销。通用机器学习原子间势的出现缓解了计算瓶颈，但将瓶颈转移到人类维度：耗时的人工流程（如输入准备和数据分析）主导了研究周期。

Method: 提出Paimon平台，通过智能体集成实现材料优化和纳米模拟的自动化。在专家级液态电解质模拟上进行数百次试验，验证其抑制静默错误（看似合理但物理不正确的结果）的能力，并展示与外部科学智能体协作及自主复现文献方法的能力。

Result: Paimon显著提高了智能工作流的可靠性，有效抑制了静默错误；能够与外部科学智能体协作；可以自主从文献复现模拟方法学。

Conclusion: Paimon作为原子模拟的智能体工具集，为研究人员在整个模拟生命周期中提供了连续、以科学为中心的工作流。

Abstract: Traditionally, atomistic simulation has been constrained by the computational scaling limits of ab initio methods and the parameterization overhead of empirical force fields. The recent emergence of universal machine learning interatomic potentials has significantly mitigated these bottlenecks, offering near-quantum accuracy and generalizability across diverse chemical spaces at a fraction of the computational cost. However, this shift has relocated the bottleneck to the human dimension: time-consuming mechanical processes, such as input preparation and data analysis, now dominate the research lifecycle. We introduce Paimon, a Platform for Agentic Integration in Materials Optimization and Nanoscale-simulations. Through hundreds of trials on an expert-level liquid electrolyte simulation, we show that Paimon substantially improves the reliability of agentic workflows by suppressing silent errors: plausible yet physically incorrect results. We further demonstrate that Paimon can cooperate with an external scientific agent and autonomously reproduce simulation methodologies from the literature. As an agent harness for atomistic simulations, Paimon affords researchers a continuous, science-centric workflow throughout the entire simulation lifecycle.

</details>


### [90] [\textit{\textbf{First-principles}} description of pumped inelastic X-ray scattering: example of K-edge RIXS in graphite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09440)
*Elias Richter,Benedikt Maurer,Claudia Draxl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于第一性原理的方法，结合Kramers-Heisenberg公式、Bethe-Salpeter方程和实时含时密度泛函理论，用于预测光泵浦材料中的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS），并在石墨K-edge上验证了与实验吻合的角依赖特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种能够预测非平衡态（光泵浦）材料中RIXS谱的第一性原理计算框架，弥补当前理论方法在描述时间分辨RIXS实验方面的不足。

Method: 基于Kramers-Heisenberg公式计算双微分截面，利用多体微扰理论中的BSE结果；将非平衡载流子分布从实时含时密度泛函理论（RT-TDDFT）引入，扩展至时间域；推广RIXS实现以支持任意偏振，考虑入射和出射光的不同取向。

Result: 在石墨K-edge的RIXS模拟中，针对不同延迟时间后的非平衡载流子分布，成功观测到π和σ轨道衍生光谱区域的角依赖特性，与实验结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该第一性原理框架能够有效预测光泵浦材料中的时间分辨RIXS谱，为理解非平衡态电子结构和超快动力学提供了可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: We present an \textit{ab initio} framework for predicting resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) in optically pumped materials. Our methodology is based on the Kramers-Heisenberg formula for the double-differential cross section formulated using the results of the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) from many-body perturbation theory. To extend this approach to the time domain, we incorporate non-equilibrium charge-carrier distributions obtained from real-time, time-dependent density-functional theory (RT-TDDFT). Generalizing the RIXS implementation with respect to arbitrary polarizations, allows us to consider different orientations of incoming and outgoing light. We demonstrate our method's capabilities by studying RIXS at the K-edge of graphite for various non-equilibrium charge-carrier distributions, representing different delay times after optical pumping. Our results reveal angular dependencies in $π$- and $σ$-orbital-derived spectral regions, in good agreement with experiment.

</details>


### [91] [Controlled component segregation in vapor-deposited organic semiconductor glass mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09527)
*Shinian Cheng,Yejung Lee,Lian Yu,Mark D. Ediger,Dean M. DeLongchamp,Camille E. Bishop*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过共沉积二元有机半导体混合物TPD-TCTA，发现其在玻璃态薄膜中可实现从均匀混合到相分离的可调控组分分离行为，并揭示了动力学 arrested 的成核-生长机制，为有机电子器件中纳米/介观尺度形貌控制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分气相沉积有机玻璃在有机电子应用中至关重要，但在纳米和介观尺度上实现可控的组分分离仍是一个挑战，阻碍了高性能器件的理性开发。

Method: 采用差示扫描量热法(DSC)和共振软X射线散射(RSoXS)研究组分分离行为，并利用NIST RSoXS模拟套件结合原子力显微镜(AFM)解释能量依赖的RSoXS光谱。

Result: 共沉积TPD-TCTA薄膜中的组分分离是由动力学 arrested 的成核-生长机制导致的，与之前报道的强不混溶TPD-DO37混合物的分离机制不同。实现了从均匀混合到相分离的连续形貌调控。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了有机半导体玻璃中可调的分子聚集行为，能够获得从均匀混合到相分离的连续形貌谱，为有机电子器件的形貌优化提供了新的设计策略。

Abstract: Multicomponent vapor-deposited organic glasses are essential in organic electronic applications, but achieving controlled component segregation at the nano- and mesoscale remains a challenge, hindering the rational development of high-performance devices. In this study, we investigate binary organic semiconductor mixtures of TPD (N,N'-Bis(3-methylphenyl)-N,N'-diphenylbenzidine) and TCTA (Tris(4-carbazoyl-9-ylphenyl)amine). Despite being miscible in the bulk liquid state, the co-deposited glassy films of these two organic semiconductors exhibit a range of segregation behaviors, from homogenous to clearly phase-separated structures. We employed differential scanning calorimetry and resonant soft X-ray scattering (RSoXS) to study the component segregation behavior and used the National Institute of Standards and Technology RSoXS Simulation Suite, paired with Atomic Force Microscopy, to interpret the energy-dependent RSoXS spectra. Our results indicate that component segregation in co-deposited TPD-TCTA films is due to a kinetically-arrested nucleation-and-growth mechanism, in contrast to the segregation mechanism of a previously reported TPD-DO37 (disperse orange 37) mixture which is strongly immiscible in bulk. This work provides a demonstration of tunable molecular aggregation in organic semiconductor glasses, enabling access to a continuum of morphologies from homogeneously mixed to segregated phases.

</details>


### [92] [Predicting Physical and Physical-Chemical Properties of Molecular-Based Materials Using Computational Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09560)
*Andrei A. Gakh,Bobby G. Sumpter,Donald W. Noid*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于神经网络的分子材料性质预测方法，通过简单算法将有机分子结构编码为数值向量，利用反向传播神经网络建立结构与性质间的定量关系。对碳氢化合物、氢氟碳化合物和冠醚的热力学、物理及物理化学性质预测准确率达0.2-8.1%，最大偏差16-20%，并成功应用于聚合物材料性质估算。提出"计算合成"概念用于材料设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以直接从化学结构准确预测分子材料的多种性质，需要一种能够建立定量结构-性质关系(QSPR)的计算方法，以便高效筛选和设计新型材料。

Method: 采用简单编码算法将有机分子的结构和组成直接转化为数值向量作为神经网络输入，使用反向传播(Backpropagation)神经网络建立输入与目标性质之间的非线性映射关系。

Result: 对烃类、氢氟碳化合物和冠醚的热容、焓、蒸发热、沸点、密度、折射率、稳定常数等多种性质预测平均准确率0.2-8.1%，最大偏差16-20%；聚合物材料的物理和力学性质估算优于传统回归分析。

Conclusion: 神经网络能够有效建立准确的定量结构-性质关系(QSPR)，并由此提出"计算合成"(computational synthesis)技术，可直接基于目标性能需求进行材料分子结构的设计与优化。

Abstract: A computational scheme, which utilizes neural networks, was developed to predict properties of molecular-based materials from chemical structures. The method uses a set of simple algorithms to encode the structure and composition of organic molecules directly into numerical vectors, which is used as input for neural networks. Backpropagation type neural networks are then used to correlate these numeric inputs with a set of desired properties. Calculated results for a series of hydrocarbons, hydrofluorocarbons, and crown ethers demonstrate average accuracies of 0.2-8.1% with maximum deviations of 16-20% for a broad range of thermodynamic, physical, and physical-chemical characteristics (heat capacity, enthalpy, heat of evaporation, boiling point, density, refractive index, stability constants, etc.). In addition, a number of physical and mechanical properties were estimated for polymeric materials and compared with regression analysis. Based on the neural network capabilities of formulating accurate quantitative structure property relationships, a technique called computational synthesis is suggested for performing materials design.

</details>


### [93] [Stoichiometric Epitaxial Strontium Titanate Thin Films on Silicon by High-Temperature Sr Segregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09574)
*Andries Boelen,Marina Baryshnikova,Maxim Korytov,Sean R. C. McMitchell,Felix Cahyadi,Christian Haffner,Clement Merckling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过后生长退火方法，将故意富Sr的SrTiO₃(STO)薄膜驱向化学计量比。退火过程中存在两种Sr分离机制：低温下(＜800°C)SrO向表面偏析并升华，高温下(＞800°C)Sr在界面SiO₂层中积累。两者共同去除过量Sr，改善STO晶格质量，为高质量STO/硅集成提供了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STO薄膜在硅上生长需要精确的化学计量比和单晶有序性，但氧化物分子束外延存在源氧化和通量不稳定的问题，导致阳离子计量比控制的生长窗口很窄。因此需要寻找后处理途径来修正偏离化学计量比的问题。

Method: 在硅(001)衬底上生长富Sr的STO薄膜，随后在氧气环境中进行宽温度范围的后生长退火处理，通过分析不同温度下Sr的分布和迁移行为，研究退火对STO化学计量比的调控机制。

Result: 发现两种Sr分离机制：低于800°C时，过量Sr向表面偏析形成SrO析出物并逐渐升华；高于800°C时，Sr在STO/Si界面处的SiO₂层中积累。两种机制共同有效去除STO晶格中的过量Sr，得到更接近化学计量比的钙钛矿层。

Conclusion: 生长略微富Sr的STO模板后进行可控退火，是提高晶体质量的有效途径，为高质量STO在硅上的集成提供了可扩展的策略。

Abstract: Thin-film strontium titanate (SrTiO$_3$, STO) layers grown on silicon require accurate stoichiometry and single-crystalline order to exploit their functional properties optimally. Oxide molecular beam epitaxy can provide an epitaxial interface, but suffers from source oxidation and resulting flux instabilities, yielding only a narrow growth process window for cationic stoichiometry control. Here, we investigate post-growth annealing in oxygen as a pathway to drive the STO layer toward stoichiometry in intentionally Sr-rich epitaxial STO films on silicon (001). Annealing over a broad temperature range revealed two distinct Sr-segregation mechanisms. Below 800 °C, excess Sr segregates toward the surface, forming SrO outgrowths that progressively sublimate at elevated temperatures. Above 800 °C, a second mechanism dominates: Sr accumulates within the interfacial SiO$_2$ layer formed by oxygen diffusion at the STO/Si interface. Together, these mechanisms effectively remove excess Sr from the STO lattice, yielding a more stoichiometric perovskite layer. Our results demonstrate that growing slightly Sr-rich STO templates followed by controlled annealing provides a practical route to improve crystalline quality, offering a scalable strategy for high-quality STO integration on silicon.

</details>


### [94] [k-Means Clustering in Fingerprint-Based Configuration Selection for Fitting Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09575)
*Miroslav Lebeda,Jan Drahokoupil,Ludvík Löbel,Petr Vlčák*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于CrystalNN模型和径向分布函数(RDF)的原子构型指纹，通过k-means聚类从大数据集中选择任意数量的不同构型，用于拟合经典分子动力学势函数。与随机选择相比，该方法在更少构型下实现了更高的能量和力预测精度。以钛的嵌入原子法(EAM)势拟合为例，从1800个初始构型中，仅约30个构型即可获得描述全集的良好模型。t-SNE降维显示含与不含Ti空位的构型子集存在重叠，表明信息冗余，k-means聚类能捕捉这一特征而随机选择不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机选择构型用于拟合经典分子动力学势函数时效率低下，需要大量DFT计算数据；而现有聚类方法缺乏针对原子局部环境的有效指纹表示，难以实现构型的高效筛选和信息去冗余。

Method: 1) 基于CrystalNN模型和RDF构建原子构型指纹；2) 对指纹向量进行k-means聚类选择代表性子集；3) 拟合EAM势（钛体系）；4) 使用t-SNE将指纹降维至2D空间进行可视化分析；5) 比较k-means聚类与随机选择在不同构型数量下的精度和标准差。

Result: k-means聚类在能量和力的拟合精度及标准差上一致优于随机选择；约30个构型即可充分代表1800个构型全集；t-SNE揭示含/不含空位构型在指纹空间中重叠；排除重叠构型后预测精度不受影响，证实信息冗余性。

Conclusion: 基于CrystalNN+RDF指纹的k-means聚类方法可高效、精准地选择代表性原子构型，大幅减少拟合经典势函数所需的DFT计算量，且能自动识别信息冗余构型，为大规模原子模拟中的势函数开发提供有效工具。

Abstract: In this study, we present a method for selecting an arbitrary number of distinct configurations from a larger data set by applying k-means clustering to atomistic configuration fingerprints based on the CrystalNN model and radial distribution function (RDF). This approach improves the accuracy of fitting classical molecular dynamics interatomic potentials to density functional theory (DFT) data for both energies and forces while requiring fewer configurations than random selection. We demonstrate this improvement by fitting an embedded-atom method (EAM) potential for titanium, using various configurational sizes from an initial set of 1800 configurations. The k-means clustering consistently achieves better precision and lower standard deviations for a smaller number of configurations than random selection. The results also suggest that only about 30 configurations are sufficient to obtain an EAM model that describes well the full set of 1800 configurations in terms of energies and forces. Additionally, t-distributed stochastic neighbor embedding (t-SNE) method was used to reduce the configuration fingerprints into 2D space, and it revealed an overlap between two configuration subsets with and without Ti vacancy, indicating similar atomic environments. This similarity is captured by k-means clustering but not by random selection. Furthermore, when the overlapping configurations with vacancies were excluded from the k-means algorithm and used only as a test set, their energy and force predictions showed similar precision to those when they were included. This indicates that the overlapping configurations in the 2D t-SNE space indeed imply potential information redundancy among the atomistic configurations.

</details>


### [95] [Lattice genome: representation and analysis of heterogeneous crystalline microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09611)
*Jiayang Wang,Mathieu Calvat,J. C. Stinville,Marat I. Latypov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文受材料基因组概念启发，提出了晶体材料的"晶格基因"和"晶格基因组"。晶格基因是通过变分自编码器编码菊池衍射图谱得到的局部晶体结构紧凑表示，满足材料基因的关键标准；晶格基因组是电子背散射衍射映射的晶格基因空间分辨率集合。通过三个应用（潜在组分图、域分割、核与域潜在向量散布）验证了该方法在增材制造和锻造镍基高温合金微观结构分析中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受广义材料基因组概念的启发，旨在为晶体材料开发一种紧凑、实验可获取、包含距离度量、且信息量足够的局部结构表示方法，以捕捉影响材料性能的介观尺度异质性。

Method: 利用变分自编码器（VAE）对Kikuchi衍射图谱进行编码，得到紧凑的晶格基因表示；通过EBSD获取空间分辨的晶格基因集合构成晶格基因组；在潜在空间中应用距离和角度度量进行域分割、生成潜在组分图、计算核和域潜在向量散布。

Result: 提出的晶格基因满足紧凑性、实验可获取性、存在反映结构相似性的距离度量、足够信息量以重建衍射图谱等关键标准；三个应用工具在增材制造和锻造镍基高温合金的微观结构上得到验证，能够可视化晶粒尺度及晶粒内部异质性。

Conclusion: 晶格基因和晶格基因组为晶体材料提供了一种有效的高维异质性分析框架，其三类应用工具（潜在组分图、域分割、潜在向量散布）分别对应传统EBSD分析中的相图、晶粒分割和KAM/GOS的高维类比，为材料微观结构表征提供了新的分析维度。

Abstract: Inspired by the concept of a generalized materials genome, we introduce the notions of lattice gene and lattice genome for crystalline materials. A lattice gene is a compact representation of the local crystalline structure obtained by encoding the Kikuchi diffraction patterns with a variational autoencoder. We show that this representation satisfies key criteria for a materials gene: compactness, experimental accessibility, existence of a distance metric reflecting structural similarity, and sufficient information content for reconstructing the original diffraction patterns. The lattice genome is the spatially resolved collection of lattice genes across a representative area mapped by electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD), which captures mesoscale heterogeneity that ultimately controls properties. We demonstrate three applications of the lattice genome: (i) latent component maps that visualize grain-scale and intra-grain heterogeneities, (ii) domain segmentation based on distance and angle metrics in the latent space, and (iii) kernel and domain latent vector spreads that quantify intragranular heterogeneity as high-dimensional analogs of kernel average misorientation and grain orientation spread. All three tools are validated on microstructures of additively manufactured and wrought Ni-base superalloys in as-built and recrystallized conditions.

</details>


### [96] [Ab initio parametrization of distributed polarizable force fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09647)
*Felix Post,Jean-Philip Filling,Toulik Maitra,Falk May,Michael Wand,Denis Andrienko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出两种改进极化力场精度的方法：(1) 将原子极化率分配给单个原子而非原子类型，(2) 使用原子极化率张量替代标量值。通过第一性原理参数化流程和消息传递图神经网络预测极化率参数，在不增加模拟计算成本的前提下，提升了对阳离子、阴离子、激发态和中性分子的描述精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化力场相比经典力场具有更好的可迁移性和精度，但现有方法在分配原子极化率时仍存在精度不足的问题。本文旨在通过改进极化率的分配方式（原子级而非原子类型级）和表示形式（张量替代标量）来显著提升极化力场的准确性，并解决从头算参数化计算成本高昂的问题。

Method: (1) 提出两种改进：将原子极化率分配给单个原子（而非原子类型），使用原子极化率张量（而非标量值）；(2) 建立基于第一性原理的原子极化率张量和标量的参数化流程，在含共轭单元的小有机分子集上验证；(3) 训练消息传递图神经网络预测极化率参数，以克服从头算的高计算成本。

Result: 改进后的极化力场扩展了对阳离子、阴离子和激发态的适用性，同时提供了对中性分子更准确的描述。图神经网络方法在不增加模拟计算成本的前提下实现了高效的参数化，并提供了识别极化力场模型无法准确描述分子极化率的诊断标准。

Conclusion: 原子级极化率分配和张量表示可显著提升极化力场的精度和适用范围；结合图神经网络的预测方法能在保持计算效率的同时实现精确的参数化，为极化力场的广泛应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Polarizable force fields offer superior transferability and accuracy compared to classical force fields, enabling access to electronic response properties such as refractive index and electronic density of states. Here, we demonstrate two key improvements that significantly enhance their accuracy: (1) assigning atomic polarizability to individual atoms rather than atom types, and (2) employing atomic polarizability tensors instead of scalar values. These modifications extend the applicability of polarizable force fields to cations, anions, and excited states, while also providing more accurate descriptions of neutral molecules. We propose a first-principles-based parameterization procedure for atomic polarizability tensors and scalars, validated on a set of small organic molecules with conjugated building blocks. To overcome the computational cost of ab initio calculations, we train a message-passing graph neural network to predict polarizability parameters, enabling efficient and scalable parameterization. Crucially, this approach imposes no additional computational cost during simulations and provides a clear diagnostic criterion for identifying cases where polarizable force field models fail to accurately describe molecular polarizability.

</details>


### [97] [Bi-S network origin of cation-disorder stability and dispersive band edges in AgBiS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09704)
*Han-Pu Liang,Songyuan Geng,Heng Kang,Chen Qiu,Xiao-Ping Yao,Qing'an Li,Bozhao Zhang,Lechuan Sun,Yuxuan Chen,Shan Zhang,Su-Huai Wei,Peng-Fei Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合机器学习原子间势与深度学习哈密顿量，从大尺度上解析了无序阳离子AgBiS2的结构-电子耦合演化，揭示了三维Bi-S网络是维持无序相稳定性和决定带边电子态的关键结构基元，澄清了有序相的结构争议，建立了非等价半导体合金中无序稳定性与光电响应的统一物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阳离子无序的AgBiS2是一种有前途的无铅光电材料，但其有序结构及优异电子性质的微观起源存在争议。理论预测混合配位倾向（四面体AgS4和八面体BiS6），而实验主要报道八面体配位的有序/无序相及局域阳离子偏心位移。

Method: 结合机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential）与深度学习哈密顿量（deep-learning Hamiltonian），在大尺度上解析AgBiS2的结构与电子耦合演化。

Result: 1) 弱无序下Ag/Bi交换与Ag亚晶格偏心倾向竞争，产生强扭曲局域环境和复杂的衍射特征；2) 随无序度增加，BiS6类单元连接成连续三维Bi-S网络，稳定岩盐类无序相；3) 尽管阳离子强无序，AgBiS2仍保持清晰的半导体带色散并形成直接带隙；4) Bi-S网络支持的Bi:p-S:p态维持导带边色散和小电子有效质量；5) 可移动Ag破坏Ag-S键长程周期性，导致价带态强局域化。

Conclusion: 澄清了有序AgBiS2的结构争议，建立了非等价半导体合金中无序稳定性与光电响应的统一物理图像。

Abstract: Cation-disordered AgBiS2 is a promising lead-free optoelectronic material, but both its ordered structure and the microscopic origin of its favorable electronic properties remain debated. Theory has proposed a mixed-coordination tendency with tetrahedral AgS4 and octahedral BiS6 units, whereas experiments mainly report octahedrally coordinated ordered and cation-disordered phases, together with local cation off-centering. Here, we combine a machine-learning interatomic potential with a deep-learning Hamiltonian to resolve the coupled structural and electronic evolution of AgBiS2 at large length scales. We identify the three-dimensional Bi-S network as the central structural motif governing both disorder stability and band-edge electronic states. At weak disorder, Ag/Bi exchange competes with the off-centering tendency of the Ag sublattice, producing strongly distorted local environments and convoluted diffraction signatures that hinder the identification of the ordered phase. With increasing disorder, BiS6-like units connect into a continuous Bi-S network, which stabilizes the rocksalt-like disordered phase. Despite strong cation disorder, AgBiS2 retains clear semiconductor-like band dispersion and develops a direct band gap. The connected Bi:p-S:p states supported by the Bi-S network preserve a dispersive conduction-band edge and a small electron effective mass. In contrast, mobile Ag disrupts the long-range periodicity of Ag-S bonding, leading to strongly localized valence states. These results clarify the structural controversy in ordered AgBiS2 and establish a unified physical picture of disorder stability and optoelectronic response in nonisovalent semiconductor alloys.

</details>

### [98] [Length-resolved Operator Growth and Path-Entropy Obstructions to Many-Body Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07774)
*J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文证明无序Ising链中局域算符的高阶微扰增长呈近阶乘速率，导致算子最大程度空间离域化，排除了最强形式的多体局域化（动力学局域性），并建立了有限尺寸交叉标度L~(W/J)²，同时指出存在结构性的路径熵障碍阻碍微扰LIOM构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无序横纵场Ising链中局域算符在高阶微扰下的展开行为，检验是否存在动力学局域性（即多体局域化的最强形式），并探究有限尺寸效应对数值研究的影响。

Method: 将算子范数按支撑长度分解，证明在特征长度ℓ_k ~ k/ln k处算符权重已呈现近阶乘增长；通过路径熵分析揭示微扰LIOM构造的结构性障碍。

Result: 局域算符的高阶微扰系数在支撑长度ℓ_k处增长率为k/ln k，即||[H,σ^z_0]^{(k)}_{ℓ_k}||_2 ≳ (k/ln k)^k；有限尺寸交叉标度为L~(W/J)²；存在路径熵障碍，非共振机制即可破坏微扰LIOM构造。

Conclusion: 该模型在任何无序强度下都排除动力学局域性；数值模拟在L≲(W/J)²范围内仅能观测到前渐近行为，需谨慎解释；真实动力学可能为弹道式算子扩展，除非存在尚未发现的抵消机制。

Abstract: For the disordered Ising chain with transverse and longitudinal fields, where couplings and fields are drawn from strictly positive distributions, Cao~\cite{Cao} has shown that the moments $μ_{2k} = \|[H,σ^z_0]^{(k)}\|_2^2$ grow almost factorially, $μ_{2k}^{1/(2k)}\sim k/\ln k$, and thus asymptotically at the maximal allowed rate. We generalize this result by resolving the operator norm in support length and show that the weight at length $\ell_k \sim k/\ln k$ already exhibits almost factorial growth, $\|[H,σ^z_0]^{(k)}_{\ell_k}\|_2 \gtrsim (k/\ln k)^k$. This implies maximal spatial delocalization of local operators and, in particular, rules out dynamical locality -- the strongest form of many-body localization -- at any disorder strength. We further establish rigorously a finite-size crossover scale $L\sim (W/J)^2$, where $W$ is the disorder and $J$ the coupling strength. For $L\lesssim (W/J)^2$ numerical studies only access a pre-asymptotic regime. Finally, we identify a structural path-entropy obstruction to perturbative LIOM constructions, based on the almost factorial branching of operator content and independent of resonance effects; the same mechanism strongly suggests ballistic real-time operator spreading, so sub-ballistic or localized dynamics would require a presently unidentified cancellation principle acting on almost factorially many disorder-dependent paths with random amplitudes.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamical cavity method for continuous-time complex systems on sparse random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08689)
*Fernando L. Metz,Isaac Pérez Castillo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文从路径测度层面发展了稀疏随机图上的动力学平均场理论（DMFT），通过空穴法推导了稀疏网络上的有效随机过程，揭示了有向与互惠图在动力学闭包上的本质差异，并给出了高连接极限下的稠密DMFT对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DMFT适用于稠密高维无序动力学，但其高斯闭包机制在处理具有有限强输入的稀疏异质网络时不再成立，亟需建立适用于稀疏网络的理论框架。

Method: 采用连续时间空穴法（cavity method）在路径测度层面推导稀疏随机图上的随机动力学，区分有向图与互惠（双向）图的路径概率方程；结合系综平均和多重线性闭包得到路径概率消息的统计规律；并提出因果离散时间推导和有限记忆数值闭包方案。

Result: 有向图获得稀疏有向路径概率方程，互惠图则需要由接收节点历史条件驱动的条件路径核；系综平均下路径概率消息的矩通过多重线性和输入分支独立性实现闭包；高连接极限下稀疏路径测度理论可投影为标准稠密DMFT。

Conclusion: 稀疏网络DMFT在路径测度层面可精确建立，互惠性从根本上改变动力学闭包结构，高连接极限提供了连接稀疏与稠密DMFT的统一理论视角。

Abstract: Dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reduces dense high-dimensional disordered dynamics to a self-consistent effective stochastic process. For sparse and heterogeneous networks, however, local fields contain finitely many strong inputs, so the Gaussian closure mechanisms of dense DMFT need not apply. We develop a continuous-time cavity derivation of sparse-network DMFT at the level of path measures for stochastic dynamics with general pairwise interactions on sparse random graphs. The cavity equations are exact on trees and yield the finite-time thermodynamic description on locally tree-like graphs. They show explicitly how reciprocity changes dynamical closure: fully directed graphs recover the sparse directed path-probability equation, whereas reciprocal or bidirected edges require conditional path kernels driven by the imposed history of the receiving node. Ensemble averaging gives laws over path-probability messages, with barycenters and higher-message moments closing by multilinearity and independence of incoming branches. A causal discrete-time derivation yields the corresponding population-dynamics representation, distinguishing trajectory populations for directed graphs from conditional branch-law or finite-depth tree populations for reciprocal graphs. We also formulate finite-memory numerical closures and test them in an additive-input recurrent neural network specialization. Finally, high-connectivity limits are obtained as projections of the sparse path-measure theory, clarifying when dense drift, noise, and response channels reduce to standard low-dimensional DMFT and when path-level descriptions remain essential.

</details>


### [100] [Statistical Mechanics of Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08706)
*Isaac Pérez Castillo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文是基于2024年春季学院讲座整理的讲义，系统介绍了从统计力学视角研究稀疏和稀释随机矩阵谱理论的方法，涵盖空腔法、复制法、预解技术、群体动力学、典型谱密度、谱计数涨落与大偏差、条件谱及非厄米推广等内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为随机矩阵的谱理论提供一条统计力学路径，特别是针对稀疏和稀释随机矩阵，通过讲义形式系统化地介绍相关方法与应用。

Method: 采用空腔法（cavity method）和复制法（replica method）、预解技术（resolvent techniques）、群体动力学（population dynamics）等统计力学工具，分析典型谱密度、谱计数涨落与大偏差、条件谱，并拓展至非厄米情形。

Result: 提供了稀疏和稀释随机矩阵谱理论的一套系统性讲义，囊括了核心方法及其在多种谱问题中的应用，并补充了超出讲座内容的材料以增强连贯性。

Conclusion: 本文作为讲义而非综述，选材有侧重且偏向作者自身工作，但尽量与更广泛的文献接轨；遗漏或简要处理的部分属教学目的所致，并非有意忽略。

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on the lectures on \emph{Statistical Mechanics of Random Matrices} delivered at the Spring College on the Physics of Complex Systems, held at the Abdus Salam International Centre for Theoretical Physics, Trieste, Italy, from 19 February to 15 March 2024. Their aim is to present a statistical-mechanics route to the spectral theory of sparse and diluted random matrices, with emphasis on cavity and replica methods, resolvent techniques, population dynamics, typical spectral densities, spectral-count fluctuations and large deviations, conditioned spectra, and non-Hermitian extensions. The written form of the notes has been deliberately expanded beyond the material actually covered during the lectures. This is partly because a set of lecture notes can afford a more systematic development than a sequence of blackboard lectures, and partly because several natural continuations of the material become clearer once the central methods have been introduced. Consequently, not every topic discussed here was presented during the College. The additional material is included to give a more coherent account of the subject and to indicate directions that, hopefully, can be covered in greater detail in future lectures or schools. Since these are lecture notes rather than a state-of-the-art review, the choice of topics is necessarily selective and is naturally tilted towards the author's own work and collaborations on this subject. I have nevertheless tried, within the limits of this format, to place the material in contact with the broader literature and to represent the surrounding state of the art as fairly as possible. Inevitably, some relevant contributions may be missing or treated too briefly; such omissions are unintentional and reflect the pedagogical scope of the notes rather than a judgement on their importance.

</details>


### [101] [Predictable Mean-Field Chaos in Random Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08805)
*Alkesh Yadav,Vladimir Shaidurov,Jonathan Kadmon*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明，在具有足够快傅里叶衰减解析非线性的随机递归网络中，动态平均场理论不仅是系综描述，更是对单个轨迹的条件预测理论。通过将功率谱展开到Krylov状态空间，揭示了平均场混沌中潜在确定性的层级组织，并建立了Krylov增长率与预测复杂度、李雅普诺夫指数之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态平均场理论中有效随机过程是否仅源于系综平均的问题，证明该随机性只是表象，平均场理论实际可为单个轨迹提供条件性预测。

Method: (1) 利用解析非线性函数的快速傅里叶衰减性质证明已实现平均场轨迹的连续过去唯一决定其未来；(2) 将功率谱展开到Krylov状态空间，揭示潜在确定性在无限时间模态层级中的组织方式；(3) 将Krylov增长思想从哈密顿混沌动力学扩展到经典耗散系统。

Result: Krylov增长率决定了有限分辨率预测的复杂度，并为此类网络中最大李雅普诺夫指数提供了上界；微观敏感性与预测复杂性是平均场混沌的两个不同方面。

Conclusion: 动态平均场理论不仅描述系综行为，还能对单个轨迹进行条件预测；微观敏感性与预测复杂性是平均场混沌的独立概念；Krylov增长思想可推广至经典耗散系统。

Abstract: Dynamical mean-field theory recasts deterministic chaos in random recurrent networks as an effective stochastic process. We show that for analytic nonlinearities with sufficiently fast Fourier decay, this stochasticity is only apparent: the continuous past of a realized mean-field trajectory uniquely determines its future. The mean-field theory is therefore not merely an ensemble description, but a conditional prediction theory for individual trajectories. Unfolding the power spectrum into a Krylov state space exposes how this latent determinism is organized across an infinite hierarchy of temporal modes. The associated Krylov growth rate sets the complexity of finite-resolution prediction and upper-bounds the largest Lyapunov exponent in this class of networks. Thus, microscopic sensitivity and predictive complexity are distinct aspects of mean-field chaos. Our results extend Krylov growth ideas developed for Hamiltonian chaotic dynamics to classical dissipative systems.

</details>

### [102] [Phase Formation and Thermal Stability of Superconducting Platinum Silicide Thin Films on Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07848)
*Tharanga R. Nanayakkara,Ananya Chattaraj,Mingzhao Liu,Charles T. Black*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 系统研究了PtSi薄膜的退火温度和时间对相形成、微结构和界面质量的影响，发现600°C快速热退火可在数分钟内形成纯相PtSi，且界面粗糙化源于Pt2Si向PtSi转化而非高温或长时间退火，建立了硅基超导器件的稳健工艺窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PtSi薄膜因其与CMOS工艺兼容、空气稳定且在1K附近具有超导转变温度，有望用于硅基超导量子器件，但缺乏系统的工艺-结构-性能关系研究。

Method: 采用掠入射X射线衍射、X射线反射率及电输运测量，系统表征不同退火温度（300-600°C）和持续时间（30秒至长时间）下PtSi薄膜的相形成、微结构和界面质量。

Result: 600°C快速热退火数分钟内即可形成纯相PtSi且长时间退火稳定；300-600°C范围内30秒退火可得到等效薄膜质量；界面粗糙化是Pt2Si向PtSi转化步骤的固有结果，而非高温或长时间退火导致。

Conclusion: 建立了PtSi在硅基超导器件制造中的稳健工艺窗口，为CMOS兼容的超导量子器件提供了工艺指导。

Abstract: Platinum silicide (PtSi) thin films are promising for silicon-based superconducting quantum devices due to their compatibility with CMOS fabrication, air stability, and superconducting transition temperature near 1 K. We report a systematic study of PtSi phase formation, microstructure, and interface quality as a function of annealing temperature and duration, characterizing films using grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction, X-ray reflectivity, and electrical transport measurements. Phase-pure PtSi forms within minutes by rapid thermal processing at 600 °C and is stable under extended annealing, while 30 s anneals across 300-600 °C yield equivalent film quality with consistent microstructure and superconducting properties. X-ray reflectivity reveals that interfacial roughening is an intrinsic consequence of the Pt2Si-to-PtSi conversion step rather than a result of elevated temperature or prolonged annealing. These results establish a robust processing window for PtSi formation in silicon-based superconducting device fabrication flows.

</details>


### [103] [Superconductivity in the high-pressure tetragonal phase of UTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07903)
*Yuhang Deng,Gabriel Mee,Tyler Wannamaker,Keke Feng,Mingyu Xu,Weiwei Xie,M. Brian Maple*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过高压电输运和磁性测量，确定了UTe2在高压四方相中的超导相图。超导在约5 GPa处随正交-四方相变出现，在6 GPa达到最大Tc约4 K，随后随压力下降并在18 GPa附近消失。正交相中巨大的上临界场超过Pauli顺磁极限，被认为是自旋三重态非常规超导；而四方相Hc2较小，受轨道极限约束，更接近常规超导。两种超导区域形成鲜明对比：正交相中狭窄脆弱的非常规超导区 vs 四方相中宽大稳健的常规超导区，这与U二聚体在正交相中存在而四方相中缺失的机制相一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定UTe2在高压四方相中的超导相图（Tc vs P），并探究其超导性质与正交相中超导性的差异，揭示压力诱导的从非常规到常规超导的转变机制。

Method: 在高达约16 GPa的压力下对UTe2进行电输运和磁性测量，测量超导转变温度Tc作为压力的函数，同时测量上临界场Hc2和电阻率ρ(T)的温度依赖性。

Result: 超导在约5 GPa随正交-四方结构相变出现；四方相中Tc在6 GPa达到最大值约4 K，后随压力下降，约18 GPa消失；四方相Hc2(0)≈1.2 T，小于Pauli顺磁极限，受轨道极限约束，相干长度ξ≈16.5 nm；正常态电阻率在200-240 K出现膝状特征，可能与磁有序转变有关。

Conclusion: UTe2的压力-温度相图呈现显著二分性：正交相中为狭窄、脆弱的非常规自旋三重态超导；四方相中为宽大、稳健、更常规的超导穹顶。这一二分性与U二聚体在正交相存在而四方相缺失的机制一致，表明U二聚体可能在自旋三重态超导中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Electrical transport and magnetic measurements have been made on UTe2 under pressure P up to approximately 16 GPa to determine the superconducting transition temperature Tc vs P phase diagram in the high-pressure tetragonal phase. Superconductivity emerges near 5 GPa, coincident with the orthorhombic to tetragonal phase transition; in the tetragonal phase, Tc reaches a maximum value of approximately 4 K at 6 GPa and then decreases with P and appears to vanish near 18 GPa. Tetragonal UTe2 has a relatively small upper critical field Hc2(0) $\approx$ 1.2 T at 5.3 GPa, smaller than the Pauli paramagnetic limit, and is orbitally limited with a coherence length $ξ$tetra $\approx$ 16.5 nm. This small value of Hc2(0) favors more conventional superconductivity; in contrast, the large values of Hc2(T) for orthorhombic UTe2 exceed the Pauli paramagnetic limit in all three crystallographic directions and have been attributed to unconventional superconductivity, widely believed to involve spin-triplet pairing. The temperature-pressure phase diagram of UTe2 shows a striking dichotomy: a narrow, fragile, unconventional superconducting region in the orthorhombic phase vs a broad, robust, and more conventional superconducting dome in the tetragonal phase. This dichotomy is consistent with the proposal that U-dimers, present (absent) in the orthorhombic (tetragonal) phase, may play a role in spin-triplet superconductivity of orthorhombic UTe2. In the tetragonal phase, the normal-state electrical resistivity $ρ$(T) exhibits metallic behavior with a knee between 200 and 240 K that depends weakly on P and most likely marks the onset of a transition to a magnetically ordered phase that coexists with superconductivity.

</details>


### [104] [Fragile electron-phonon superconductivity in MnB4 under pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07930)
*Renhai Wang,Shiya Chen,Feng Zheng,Zhen Zhang,Xingzhi Wang,Huafeng Dong,Cai-Zhuang Wang,Vladimir Antropov,Kai-Ming Ho,Yang Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过将Mn二聚体距离作为关键结构参数，发现MnB4中压力诱导的超导电性源于电子-声子耦合，计算得到的Tc与实验一致，表明MnB4是一个高响应性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnB4中压力诱导超导电性的起源尚不清楚，需要对其微观机制进行解释。

Method: 将体积和Mn二聚体距离作为关键结构参数来映射结构空间，分析电子-声子耦合效应。

Result: Mn二聚体距离的微小变化显著影响电子和声子性质，使计算Tc与实验吻合。

Conclusion: MnB4是一个高响应性系统，为探究结构不稳定性、超导电性和磁性之间的微妙相互作用提供了平台。

Abstract: The origin of pressure-induced superconductivity in MnB4 remains unclear. Here we show that it can be explained by electron-phonon coupling once the structural space is mapped using both volume and the Mn dimer distance as key structural parameters under compression. Minor changes in the dimer distance significantly affect electronic and phonon properties, bringing the calculated Tc into agreement with experiment. Our results suggest that MnB4 is a highly responsive system, providing a platform for probing the subtle interplay between structural instability, superconductivity and magnetism.

</details>


### [105] [Microscopic mechanism of high-temperature superconductivity revealed by ab initio studies on hole-doped multilayer cuprates HgBa$_2$Ca$_2$Cu$_3$O$_8$ under pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08181)
*Ryui Kaneko,Masatoshi Imada*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过从头算哈密顿量结合神经网络变分求解器，系统研究了三层铜酸盐超导体HgBa₂Ca₂Cu₃O₈（Hg1223）的高温超导机制，揭示了压强下d波超导序参量和T_c的穹顶行为，阐明了局域库仑排斥U、电子跳跃t及非局域库仑排斥V随压强的协同演化对超导增强的作用，指出配对机制源于“排斥减弱产生吸引”的瞬时局域吸引，并与反铁磁序共存，为新型超导材料设计提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hg1223在常压下具有已知材料中最高的超导临界温度T_c~134K，且加压后T_c可升至~160K，但其微观机制尚不明确。本文旨在通过第一性原理计算揭示其高温超导的微观起源。

Method: 采用从头算哈密顿量结合神经网络辅助的变分求解器，对Hg1223进行数值求解，系统分析了压强对d波超导序参量、T_c以及各相互作用参数（电子跳跃t、局域库仑排斥U、非局域库仑排斥V）的影响。

Result: d波超导序参量和T_c随压强呈穹顶形变化，与实验定性一致；常压下强超导振幅归因于弱屏蔽导致的强局域库仑排斥U；加压下T_c升高源于电子跳跃t增大、U减小以及更重要的非局域库仑排斥V显著降低；超导配对机制为局域排斥U经掺杂后生成的瞬时局域吸引，即“排斥减弱产生吸引”；同时观测到超导与反铁磁序共存。

Conclusion: Hg1223的超导配对源于Mott绝缘体中“假真空”态（受抑双占据）经载流子掺杂后释放所产生的瞬时局域吸引，区别于传统BCS型玻色子媒介配对机制。该微观理解为利用这种涌现局域吸引设计和优化超导材料提供了新途径。

Abstract: Triple-layer cuprate superconductor $\mathrm{HgBa_2Ca_2Cu_3O_8}$ (Hg1223) keeps the record of the highest superconducting (SC) critical temperature $T_{c}\sim 134$K among all the existing materials at ambient pressure. $T_{c}$ further increases under pressure up to $T_{c}\sim 160$K. However, its microscopic mechanism remains to be elucidated. We solve {\it ab initio} Hamiltonians for Hg1223 using a variational solver supplemented by a neural network. The pressure dependence of the $d$-wave SC order parameter and estimated $T_{c}$ show a dome-like structure in essential agreement with the experimental indications. The origin of the strong SC amplitude at ambient pressure is identified as strong local Coulomb repulsion $U$ attributed to poor screening. Further increase in $T_{c}$ under pressure is understood from interplay of three elements, namely increased electron hopping $t$, decreased $U$ and more importantly, strongly reduced offsite Coulomb repulsion $V$ with increasing pressure. Pairing mechanism is identified as the emergent local attraction counterintuitively generated from the originally strong local repulsion $U$. The emergent attraction is interpreted from ``attraction from reduced repulsion'', originating from the release of the fluctuating doubly-occupied sites characterized from the ``false vacuum'' in the Mott insulator to the double-occupation-free $d$-wave SC states upon carrier doping. This instantaneous attraction is in contrast with the conventional BCS SC mediated by bosonic glues. The local attraction is consistent with the electron fractionalization supported in experimental analyses. The coexistence of the SC and antiferromagnetic order is also demonstrated as a characteristic feature of the multi-layer system. The microscopic understanding of Hg1223 offers a new route explicitly using this emergent attraction to design and optimize SC materials.

</details>


### [106] [Nearly ballistic transport and high magnetic-field sensitivity in a $\text{Bi}_4\text{Br}_4$ topological Josephson weak link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08333)
*Enamul Haque,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Nb-1D Bi₄Br₄-Nb约瑟夫森结的弱连接器件，利用拓扑绝缘体边缘态实现弹道输运，用于高效磁传感。通过第一性原理计算和DFPT分析，该器件展现出高前向偏斜度（+1.74）的非正弦电流-相位关系及磁场相位敏感性，是片上超导磁传感器的有前景平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高透明度的超导弱连接因其相依赖的Andreev束缚态谱能产生强磁通-信号响应且耗散极小，是设计紧凑磁传感器的理想方案。利用拓扑绝缘体边缘态实现弹道输运，可克服背散射，为开发高效磁传感的弱连接器件提供独特路径。

Method: 使用超导Nb电极和Bi₄Br₄纳米带形成Nb-1D Bi₄Br₄-Nb约瑟夫森结；基于DFT计算构建第一性原理紧束缚哈密顿量和Wannier基下的轨道分辨界面耦合（含自旋-轨道耦合）；通过DFPT计算Nb的Eliashberg谱函数。

Result: Nb的电子-声子耦合强度为1.19，超导转变温度约9 K；亚隙电导主要由Andreev过程主导；ABS谱产生非正弦电流-相位关系，前向偏斜度高达+1.74，且对磁场具有相位敏感性。

Conclusion: Nb-1D Bi₄Br₄-Nb弱连接是用于片上超导磁传感器的有前景平台，兼容可扩展纳米制造和拓扑-超导混合电子学的发展。

Abstract: Superconducting weak links with high transparency offer an appealing approach for designing compact magnetic-field sensors, as their phase-dependent Andreev bound-state (ABS) spectrum produces a strong flux-to-signal response with minimal dissipation. One way to achieve ballistic transport in the weak link is to use the edge states of topological insulators, since these states resist backscattering and provide a unique path to developing topological insulator-based weak-link devices for highly efficient magnetic-field sensing. Building on this, we propose a weak-link device using superconducting Nb electrodes and a nanoribbon of $\text{Bi}_4\text{Br}_4$ as the normal region, forming a Nb-1D (one-dimensional) $\text{Bi}_4\text{Br}_4$-Nb Josephson junction. We develop first-principles tight-binding Hamiltonians and orbital-resolved interface couplings in the Wannier basis, including spin-orbit coupling, based on density functional theory (DFT) calculations. The Eliashberg spectral function of bulk Nb, obtained via density functional perturbation theory (DFPT), indicates an electron-phonon coupling strength of 1.19 and a transition temperature of about 9 K, aligning well with conventional superconductivity in Nb. The subgap conductance is primarily influenced by Andreev processes. The ABS spectrum leads to a non-sinusoidal current-phase relation (CPR) with high forward skewness ($+$1.74) and phase sensitivity to the magnetic field. Overall, our findings suggest that the Nb-1D $\text{Bi}_4\text{Br}_4$-Nb weak link is a promising platform for on-chip superconducting magnetic sensors, compatible with scalable nanofabrication and broader development of topological-superconductor hybrid electronics.

</details>


### [107] [First-Principles Investigation of Electron--Phonon Coupling and Intrinsic Two-Gap Superconductivity in Hexagonal BAs3 Monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08423)
*Jakkapat Seeyangnok,Udomsilp Pinsook*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和全各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，预测六方BAs₃单层是一种本征的、全能隙无节点的各向异性两带超导体，其超导临界温度T_c=3.4 K，具有两个超导能隙Δ₁=0.75 meV和Δ₂=0.51 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多带电子结构的二维超导体为探索维度缩减极限下的各向异性和多带超导性提供了理想平台，但此类材料仍然稀缺，需要发现新的候选体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算结合密度泛函微扰理论和全各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg理论，系统研究了六方BAs₃单层的结构、电子、振动和超导性质，并通过声子计算和从头算分子动力学模拟验证了其动力学和热力学稳定性。

Result: 电子结构显示本征金属态，多带穿越费米能级；电声耦合常数λ=0.75，主要来自低频As衍生声子模；各向异性Eliashberg方程求解得T_c=3.4 K；动量分辨超导能隙呈现明显两带特征，Δ₁=0.75 meV和Δ₂=0.51 meV（T=1 K），整个费米面上能隙有限，为全能隙无节点超导态；两带超导起源于不同费米面片相关的配对相互作用。

Conclusion: Monolayer BAs₃被确立为一种本征各向异性两带超导体，扩展了硼基二维超导体家族。

Abstract: Two-dimensional superconductors with multiband electronic structures provide an ideal platform for exploring anisotropic and multigap superconductivity in the reduced-dimensionality limit. Here, we investigate the structural, electronic, vibrational, and superconducting properties of a hexagonal BAs$_3$ monolayer using first-principles calculations combined with density functional perturbation theory and fully anisotropic Migdal--Eliashberg theory. The optimized structure is found to be dynamically and thermally stable, as confirmed by phonon calculations and ab initio molecular dynamics simulations. Electronic structure calculations reveal an intrinsic metallic state with multiple bands crossing the Fermi level and several disconnected Fermi-surface sheets derived primarily from hybridized B-$p$ and As-$p$ orbitals. The electron--phonon interaction is dominated by low-frequency As-derived phonon modes, yielding a total electron--phonon coupling constant of $λ=0.75$. Solving the anisotropic Eliashberg equations predicts a superconducting critical temperature of $T_c=3.4$ K. The momentum-resolved superconducting gap exhibits a pronounced two-gap character with gap magnitudes of $Δ_1=0.75$ meV and $Δ_2=0.51$ meV at $T=1$ K. The superconducting gaps remain finite over the entire Fermi surface, demonstrating a fully gapped nodeless superconducting state. Analysis of the momentum-dependent electron--phonon coupling reveals that the two-gap superconductivity originates from sheet-dependent pairing interactions associated with distinct Fermi-surface sheets. Our results establish monolayer BAs$_3$ as an intrinsic anisotropic two-gap superconductor and expand the growing family of boron-based two-dimensional superconductors.

</details>


### [108] [Coexistence of High Temperature Superconductivity and Antiferromagnetic Order in a Cuprate with Multiple Hole Fermi Pockets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08643)
*Xiangyu Luo,Yinghao Li,Hao Chen,Yiwen Chen,Jumin Shi,Taimin Miao,Bo Liang,Wenpei Zhu,Neng Cai,Xiaolin Ren,Yingjie Shu,Chaohui Yin,Jiuxiang Zhang,Chengtian Lin,Shenjin Zhang,Zhimin Wang,Fengfeng Zhang,Feng Yang,Qinjun Peng,Zuyan Xu,Guodong Liu,Xintong Li,Hanqing Mao,Tao Xiang,Lin Zhao,X. J. Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用高分辨率激光角分辨光电子能谱研究了七层Bi2267铜氧化物，发现了一个由多个空穴费米口袋构成的系统。这些费米口袋表现出显著的动量、温度和费米面依赖的能隙。关键在于，在具有多个费米口袋且存在强反铁磁序和关联的体系中，观察到了约75K的高温超导电性，尤其在轻掺杂CuO₂面（p~0.05）沿费米口袋观测到高达42 meV的电子配对能隙。这一发现挑战了节点和反节点电子态驱动高温超导的传统认知，表明超导与反铁磁可以在多费米口袋铜氧化物中共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决铜氧化物中高温超导与反铁磁序之间复杂的相互作用关系，以及电子配对的基本起源这两个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 采用高分辨率激光基空间分辨角分辨光电子能谱（ spatially-resolved ARPES）对七层Bi₂Sr₂Ca₆Cu₇O₁₈₊δ（Bi2267）体系进行测量分析。

Result: 发现Bi2267是一个由多个空穴费米口袋构成的体系；费米口袋表现出动量、温度和费米面依赖的能隙；在存在强反铁磁序和关联的多费米口袋体系中观察到了~75 K的高温超导转变；在轻掺杂（p~0.05）的CuO₂面中沿费米口袋观测到高达~42 meV的显著电子配对能隙。

Conclusion: 这些发现挑战了节点和反节点电子态在驱动高温超导中作用的传统理解，证明了超导与反铁磁可以在多费米口袋铜氧化物中共存，为理解铜氧化物超导体的配对机制提供了新的见解。

Abstract: The intricate relationship between high temperature superconductivity and antiferromagnetic order in cuprates, and the fundamental origin of electron pairing remain open questions. By utilizing high-resolution laser-based spatially-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we investigate the seven-layer $Bi_{2}Sr_{2}Ca_{6}Cu_{7}O_{18+δ}$ (Bi2267) and identify a cuprate system that consists of multiple hole Fermi pockets. The observed Fermi pockets exhibit pronounced momentum-, temperature- and Fermi surface-dependent energy gaps. Crucially, high temperature superconductivity with a critical temperature ($T_{\mathrm{c}}$) of $\sim$75 K emerges in a system with multiple Fermi pockets and the presence of strong antiferromagnetic order and correlations. In particular, substantial electron pairing is observed along the Fermi pocket with an energy gap up to $\sim$42 meV in lightly-doped CuO$_{2}$ planes ($p\sim$0.05). These findings challenge the conventional understanding of the roles of the nodal and antinodal electronic states in driving high-temperature superconductivity. They show that superconductivity and antiferromagnetism can coexist in a cuprate with multiple Fermi pockets, offering further insights into the pairing mechanism in cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [109] [Superconducting diode effect in magnetic superconductors realized by nonreciprocal domain-wall dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08999)
*Dong Hui Han,Suk Bum Chung,Se Kwon Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文揭示了铁磁超导体中磁畴壁的非互易Walker击穿可映射为超导二极管效应，使拓扑缺陷成为实现纳米级超导非互易功能的内在平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在单一均匀材料中实现超导二极管效应的内在机制，避免复杂异质结构工程，推动超导器件纳米化。

Method: 理论分析磁畴壁在外场下电流驱动动力学的非互易Walker击穿，结合微磁模拟验证，并扩展至包含超导效应的铁磁超导体模型。

Result: 发现铁磁超导体中畴壁的持续旋转引起相位滑移，打开耗散通道，从而将非互易Walker击穿映射为超导二极管效应；解析了磁场和吉尔伯特阻尼的依赖性。

Conclusion: 磁性超导体中的拓扑缺陷（如畴壁）可作为内在的纳米级平台实现非线性非互易超导功能，无需复杂异质结构，有望突破传统约瑟夫森结在器件小型化方面的局限。

Abstract: A superconducting diode effect is shown to arise in ferromagnetic superconductors through the nonreciprocal dynamics of magnetic domain walls. Specifically, we show that current-driven dynamics of a magnetic domain wall under a certain external field can exhibit a nonreciprocal Walker breakdown, possessing two distinct direction-dependent critical currents beyond which the domain wall precesses continuously. In ferromagnetic superconductors, the constant rotation of a domain wall is shown to give rise to phase slips, opening up dissipation channels, whereby the nonreciprocal Walker breakdown is mapped to the superconducting diode effect. For the nonreciprocal Walker breakdown of a magnetic domain wall, we analytically examine its dependence on the magnetic field and the Gilbert damping and verify the theoretical results with micromagnetic simulations. We then extend the analysis to ferromagnetic superconductors by considering additional effects from the superconductivity and identify criteria for experimental conditions to realize the predicted superconducting diode effect. Our work demonstrates that topological defects, such as domain walls, in magnetic superconductors can serve as an intrinsic nanoscale platform for nonlinear nonreciprocal superconducting functionalities within a single homogeneous material, circumventing the need for complicated engineered heterostructures and thereby enabling the miniaturization of superconducting devices down to the nanometer scale that is challenging to achieve with conventional Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [110] [Proximity-induced orbital antiferromagnetism in Ising superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09797)
*G. A. Bobkov,V. A. Bobkov,T. Karabassov,I. V. Bobkova,A. A. Golubov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文预言了超导体/反铁磁体异质结中一种全新的超导态——邻近诱导的轨道反铁磁态。该态通过Ising自旋-轨道耦合产生原子尺度的周期性相位调制，形成环形电流和交替轨道磁矩。以NbSe₂/MnPS₃为具体例子，结合第一性原理和Bogoliubov-de Gennes计算证明其稳定存在，并通过STM可观测的有限能量谷底特征来检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导体/反铁磁体异质结中由Ising自旋-轨道耦合驱动的新型超导态，揭示原子尺度相位调制机制及其物理起源。

Method: 以NbSe₂/MnPS₃为具体体系，结合第一性原理计算和Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程进行理论建模与分析。

Result: 发现邻近诱导的交换场导致稳健的原子尺度相位调制；该态具有载流能力，在全体参数范围内唯一稳定；在局域态密度中表现为有限能量处的谷底特征。

Conclusion: 预言了一种由轨道反铁磁性驱动的全新超导态，其物理机制与FFLO态和螺旋态本质不同，可通过STM实验探测。

Abstract: We predict a fundamentally new superconducting state in superconductor/antiferromagnet heterostructures with Ising spin--orbit coupling: proximity-induced orbital antiferromagnetism. In this state, the order parameter acquires a periodic phase modulation locked to the magnetic lattice, generating atomic-scale loop currents with opposite orbital moments on neighboring unit cells. Its emergence requires at least three nonequivalent magnetic sublattices per unit cell and finite spin--orbit coupling. Using NbSe$_2$/MnPS$_3$ as a concrete example, we combine first-principles and Bogoliubov--de Gennes calculations to demonstrate that the proximity-induced exchange field leads to robust phase modulation. Unlike FFLO and helical states, the phase gradient is atomic-scale, the state is current-carrying, and it remains uniquely stable over the full parameter range. The state manifests as characteristic finite-energy dips in the local density of states, accessible by STM.

</details>

### [111] [A Finite-Lattice Model from a Reciprocal Cost Action: Spectral and Reflection-Positivity Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07922)
*Jonathan Washburn,Megan Simons*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个有限晶格统计力学模型，其最近邻键势为$J(e^\varepsilon)=\cosh\varepsilon-1$。针对连续非紧模型，证明了自然时间核$K(u)=\exp[-(\cosh u-1)]$不满足Bochner正定性（$\widetilde K(3)<0$），从而无法通过标准Bochner路径建立Osterwalder-Schrader反射正性。而对于有限字母变体（场值限制在对称有限集$Φ=v_0\{-N,\ldots,N\}$），当有限跨越键Toeplitz矩阵正定时反射正性成立；对$v_0\in\{1.2,1.5,2.5\}$，通过对角占优证书在$N$上一致地证明了这一点，相应的单步转移算子在显式反射正性内积下是正自伴的。这些有限体积结果并不提供连续Wightman理论、Osterwalder-Schrader重构、LSZ散射或连续质量间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究da' Alembert函数方程在指定正则性和标定假设下所选择的特定键势模型，探讨其反射正性（reflection positivity）成立与否的条件，这是构造性量子场论和统计力学中建立欧几里得框架的核心性质。

Method: 采用严格的数学分析：对于连续非紧模型，使用区间认证求积（interval-certified quadrature）计算$\widetilde K(3)$的数值符号来判定Bochner正定性；对于有限字母变体，通过分析跨越键Toeplitz矩阵$(K_{Φ(v_0,N)})_{a,b}=K(b-a)$的正半定性，并利用对角占优（diagonal-dominance）证书给出统一于$N$的严格证明。

Result: 连续非紧模型的自然时间核$K(u)=\exp[-(\cosh u-1)]$的傅里叶变换在$3$处取负值，不满足Bochner正定性，反射正性受阻。有限字母变体在$v_0\in\{1.2,1.5,2.5\}$时，通过一致对角占优证书证明了矩阵正半定性，从而反射正性成立，转移算子正自伴。

Conclusion: 该模型在连续非紧情形下无法通过标准Bochner路径获得Osterwalder-Schrader反射正性；但在有限字母离散化变体下，对于特定参数范围反射正性可以严格成立。然而这些有限体积结果并不自动推广到连续Wightman理论框架，也不提供质量间隙等连续极限性质。

Abstract: We study the finite-lattice statistical-mechanical model whose nearest-neighbor bond potential is the reciprocal cost $J(e^\varepsilon)=\cosh\varepsilon-1$, selected by the d'Alembert functional equation under the stated regularity and calibration assumptions. The structural inputs are stated explicitly; once they are fixed, the analysis is rigorous mathematics about the bond action $V(Δφ)=\cosh(Δφ)-1$ on finite boxes in $\mathbb Z^3\times\mathbb Z/8\mathbb Z$. Our main result pairs a negative and a positive statement about reflection positivity. For the continuous noncompact model the natural temporal kernel $K(u)=\exp[-(\cosh u-1)]$ fails the Bochner positive-definiteness test: an interval-certified quadrature gives $\widetilde K(3)<0$. Thus the standard Bochner route to Osterwalder-Schrader reflection positivity is obstructed. For a finite-alphabet variant, with field values restricted to a finite symmetric set $Φ=v_0\{-N,\ldots,N\}$, reflection positivity holds whenever the finite crossing-bond Toeplitz matrix $(K_{Φ(v_0,N)})_{a,b}:=K(b-a), a,b\inΦ$, is positive semidefinite. For $v_0\in\{1.2,1.5,2.5\}$, this is discharged by a rigorous diagonal-dominance certificate uniform in $N$, and the associated one-step transfer operator is then positive and self-adjoint in an explicit reflection-positivity inner product. These finite-volume results do not provide a continuum Wightman theory, Osterwalder-Schrader reconstruction, LSZ scattering, or a continuum mass gap.

</details>


### [112] [Control transition in a temporally random classical spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09297)
*Elisha Shmalo,J. H. Pixley,Manas Kulkarni,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,David A. Huse*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在经典自旋链模型中理论探索了受控动力学与混沌动力学之间的相变。模型交替施加混沌哈密顿动力学和收缩性“控制映射”，控制映射将系统驱向一个在混沌动力学下不稳定的目标构型。强控制使目标构型成为全局吸引的稳定不动点；弱控制则使多体动力学对几乎所有初始态保持混沌。该相变具有混合特征：从混沌相接近转变点时，远离目标构型的自旋比例连续趋于零，空间和时间关联长度发散；但主导李雅普诺夫指数在转变处不连续，从混沌相的正值跳变为受控相的负值。证据表明该相变与存在时间随机性的有向渗流属于同一普适类，该普适类的结果满足动力学Harris判据但未饱和边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索经典多体系统中受控动力学与混沌动力学之间的相变，理解控制强度如何影响系统的混沌行为，以及该相变的普适类归属。

Method: 采用经典自旋链模型，交替施加混沌哈密顿动力学和收缩性控制映射。通过改变控制强度，分析系统从混沌相到受控相的转变，并计算自旋比例、关联长度和李雅普诺夫指数等关键量。

Result: 发现了混合特征的相变：远离目标构型的自旋比例连续趋于零，空间和时间关联长度发散，但主导李雅普诺夫指数在转变处不连续。该转变与存在时间随机性的有向渗流属于同一普适类，结果满足动力学Harris判据但未饱和边界。

Conclusion: 经典自旋链中混沌与受控动力学之间的相变具有混合临界特征，属于有向渗流普适类，为理解多体系统中的控制-混沌竞争提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We theoretically explore a phase transition between controlled and chaotic dynamics in a classical spin chain model subject to chaotic Hamiltonian dynamics and a contractive "control map", which alternate in time. The control map drives the system toward a target configuration that is an unstable fixed point under the chaotic dynamics. When the control is strong enough, the target configuration is the globally attracting stable fixed point of the dynamics; for weaker control, the many-body dynamics remains chaotic for almost all initial states. The phase transition between controlled and chaotic phases has a mixed character: As the transition is approached from the chaotic phase, the fraction of the spins that are far from the target configuration goes continuously to zero, and there are diverging spatial and temporal correlation lengths; however, the leading Lyapunov exponent is discontinuous across the transition, jumping from a positive value in the chaotic phase to a negative value in the controlled phase. We present evidence that this transition is in the same universality class as directed percolation in the presence of temporal randomness, a universality class for which we obtain results that are consistent with the dynamical Harris criterion but do not saturate the bound.

</details>


### [113] [Tracking metastable phases by complex Lee-Yang zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08004)
*Yi-Hua Dong,Ling Liu,Fang-Cheng Wang,Qi-Jun Ye,Xin-Zheng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Lee-Yang零点理论证明，亚稳态相并非“隐藏”状态，而是存在于热场的复平面中，随驱动增强可向实轴移动并分裂，从而稳定存在。研究为描述亚稳态相提供了新方案，并将周期性驱动视为复热场，为非平衡态调控提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相图分析中，亚稳态相被视为被抑制的能态而“隐藏”，作者试图证明它们实际上存在于复热场平面中，并可由Lee-Yang零点界定，从而为理解亚稳态相提供理论框架。

Method: 采用具有三高斯峰可调态密度的玩具模型和更现实的周期性驱动系统进行数值模拟，通过分析Lee-Yang零点在复平面上的行为（靠近实轴与分裂），表征亚稳态相的出现与稳定化。

Result: 随着人工参数或驱动振幅增大，束缚亚稳态相的Lee-Yang零点向实轴靠近并分裂为分离分支，标志着亚稳态相在相邻稳定相之间间隙内的出现与稳定。在驱动系统中，LYZ虚部与驱动强度相关。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了描述亚稳态相的新方案，将周期性驱动视为复热场，为理解和工程化非平衡集体态提供了新视角，并连接了Lee-Yang理论与太赫兹物质调控。

Abstract: Metastable phases (MPs) are energetically unfavorable states typically suppressed in equilibrium phase diagrams. Rather than remaining ''hidden'', we show that they exist in the complex plane of thermal fields, as regions delineated by Lee-Yang zeros (LYZs). We demonstrate this numerically in a toy model with a tunable density of states featuring three Gaussian peaks and in a more realistic periodically driven system. In both cases, as artificial parameters or drive amplitudes increase, the LYZs bounding the MP approach the real axis and split into separated branches, signaling the emergence and stabilization of the MP within the enlarged gap between two adjacent stable phases. In the driven system, the imaginary part of LYZs correlates with drive strength, linking Lee-Yang theory to terahertz matter manipulation. These findings provide a scheme to describe MPs in phase diagram analysis. By viewing periodic drives as complex thermal fields, it also offers a new perspective for understanding and engineering non-equilibrium collective states.

</details>


### [114] [Deviations from Debye's specific heat due to excess energy fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09566)
*Ralph V. Chamberlin,Sumiyoshi Abe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于时间、相位和热平均顺序的理论，解释了晶体中超出德拜T^3定律的过剩比热，该理论源于无缺陷晶体分子动力学模拟中观察到的快速能量调制，并成功修正了能量涨落与比热之间的标准关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何高纯单晶中实测比热常超出德拜T^3定律，以及分子动力学模拟中无缺陷晶体出现的过剩能量涨落现象。

Method: 基于时间平均和相位平均后再进行热平均的理论框架，受模拟和实验（表明局域激发与热库退耦）的启发，并与反常扩散进行类比。

Result: 修正了能量涨落与比热之间的标准关系，理论与模拟结果高度一致，并为大量实验测量提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅解释了晶体中的过剩比热，还可能为理解非晶材料中的过剩比热以及量子器件中的反常噪声提供基础。

Abstract: Measured specific heats often exceed Debye's T^3-law, even in high-purity single crystals. Analogous excess energy fluctuations in molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of crystals with no defects come from fast energy modulations involving next-nearest-neighbor atoms. Here, a theory is developed for these modulations, based on time- and phase-averaging followed by thermal averaging. This order of averaging is guided by evidence from the simulations and various experimental techniques showing that localized excitations are decoupled from the heat bath. Emergent nonextensivity is interpreted by analogy with anomalous diffusion. The theory modifies the standard relation between energy fluctuations and specific heat, giving good agreement with the simulations and new insight into many measurements. The theory may also provide a basis for understanding excess specific heat in amorphous materials and anomalous noise in quantum devices.

</details>


### [115] [Enhanced dumbbell diffusion in a periodic potential by the elevator effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08334)
*B. A. Kiang,H. Schiessel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过哑铃模型（两个由弹簧连接的珠子）在一维周期性势场中的分子动力学模拟，发现当弹簧平衡距离与势周期不匹配且弹簧常数足够大时，哑铃的扩散常数可超过单珠子的扩散常数，这种现象被称为“电梯效应”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由弹簧连接的双粒子系统（哑铃）在周期性势场中的扩散行为，特别是探究弹簧常数、平衡距离与势场周期性的匹配程度如何影响扩散系数，以及是否可能超越单粒子的扩散性能。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟方法，对哑铃（两个由弹簧连接的珠子）在一维周期性势场中的随机行走进行模拟，并在极限情况下与理论解析方程进行对比验证。

Result: 当哑铃的平衡距离与势场周期不匹配（不可公度），且弹簧常数足够大时，哑铃的扩散常数可以超过单个珠子在同一势场中的扩散常数。

Conclusion: 发现了“电梯效应”：哑铃类似于牵引电梯，两个连接的珠子分别扮演电梯轿厢和对重，通过这种耦合机制实现增强扩散。

Abstract: We present molecular dynamics simulations of the random walk of a dumbbell - two beads connected by a spring - in a one-dimensional periodic potential and compare our results in limiting cases to theoretical analytical equations. The relevant parameters in this system are the spring constant, the equilibrium distance of the spring (relative to the periodicity of the potential), and the amplitude of the potential. Dumbbells with equilibrium distances incommensurate with the potential periodicity and with a sufficiently large spring constant exhibit enhanced diffusion. The diffusion constant can exceed that of a single bead in the same potential landscape. In this case, the dumbbell resembles a traction elevator, with the two connected beads acting as the elevator car and counterweight: the ''elevator effect''.

</details>


### [116] [A spectral model of power-law decay in natural and engineered systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08342)
*Balázs Sándor,Márk Honti,Henrique Santos Lima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种第一性原理谱机制，用于解释非理想输运系统中非广延q-指数稀释和幂律弛豫现象。通过将不完全混合反应器建模为具有吸收边界的层状扩散矩阵，证明宏观幂律尾依赖于初始示踪剂放置与边界构型之间的几何相互作用。在一维系统中，非对称体积分布的初始浓度剖面投影到低波数本征模上，产生弛豫率的Gamma分布；在边界层厚度趋近于零时，该剖面精确产生q=5/3的非广延q-指数衰减函数。扩展到d维时，标度指数和对应的q值依赖于吸收边界的空间构型，但在d=1极限下，不同初始状态和边界形式均收敛于几何不变的q=5/3指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立线性扩散输运与非广延统计力学之间的明确联系，解释重尾输运如何从边界几何和谱维度中衍生出来，为非理想输运系统中的反常弛豫现象提供第一性原理的理论基础。

Method: 将不完全混合反应器建模为具有吸收边界的层状扩散矩阵，通过分析初始示踪剂浓度剖面的谱投影，推导弛豫率的Gamma分布，并在边界层厚度趋近于零的极限下得到q-指数衰减函数。将模型从一维推广到d维，分析不同边界构型对标度指数和q值的影响。

Result: 在一维系统中，非对称体积分布的初始浓度剖面在边界层厚度趋近于零时精确产生q=5/3的非广延q-指数衰减函数。在d维推广中，标度指数和q值依赖于吸收边界的空间构型，但在一维极限下不同初始状态和边界形式均收敛于q=5/3的几何不变指数。

Conclusion: 揭示了线性扩散输运中重尾现象的几何本质，建立了扩散输运与非广延统计力学之间的理论桥梁，为理解非理想输运系统中的反常弛豫行为提供了新的谱机制视角。

Abstract: We present a first-principles spectral mechanism for the emergence of nonextensive $q$-exponential dilution and power-law relaxation in non-ideal transport systems. By modeling an incompletely mixed reactor as a layered diffusion matrix with an absorbing boundary, we demonstrate that macroscopic power-law tails depend on the geometric interaction between the initial tracer placement and the domain's boundary configuration. For a one-dimensional system, an asymmetric, volumetrically distributed initial concentration profile projects onto the low-wavenumber eigenmodes, generating an emergent Gamma distribution of relaxation rates; at an infinitesimal boundary layer thickness ($Δz \to 0$), this profile yields the nonextensive $q$-exponential decay function exactly across the entire temporal domain with $q = 5/3$. Extended to $d$ dimensions under a highly localized, boundary-adjacent singular initial condition, the resulting scaling exponents and corresponding $q$ values depend explicitly on the spatial configuration of the absorbing boundaries. However, in the one-dimensional limit ($d=1$), these distinct initial states and boundary formulations intersect, rendering the $q=5/3$ exponent geometrically invariant. Our approach establishes a clear connection between linear diffusion transport and nonextensive statistical mechanics, showing how heavy-tailed transport can be derived from boundary geometry and spectral dimensionality.

</details>


### [117] [What Is a Pattern in Statistical Mechanics? Formalizing Structure and Patterns in One-Dimensional Spin Lattice Models with Computational Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08509)
*Omar Aguilar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用信息论和计算力学方法，形式化定义了三种一维自旋晶格模型（有限程伊辛模型、固-固模型和三体模型）中的结构与模式，推导了玻尔兹曼分布并将其重构为随机过程，通过计算力学中的超额熵、统计复杂度和epsilon-机器量化了模型的结构与生成机制，并与统计力学的结果进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在用信息论和计算力学的理论框架，形式化地定义和量化一维自旋晶格模型中的"结构"与"模式"概念，弥补传统统计力学在结构量化方面的不足。

Method: 首先对嵌入无限构型中的有限一维自旋构型重新推导玻尔兹曼分布；然后将该分布重构为随机过程；接着利用计算力学理论，通过超额熵（可预测信息）量化结构、统计复杂度（存储信息）衡量结构存储成本、epsilon-机器描述结构生成机制；最后将信息度量与epsilon-机器联合确定的构型与玻尔兹曼分布下的典型构型进行比较验证。

Result: 信息度量与epsilon-机器联合确定的构型与玻尔兹曼分布下的典型构型一致，验证了计算力学框架与统计力学在该问题上的兼容性。

Conclusion: 计算力学框架能够有效量化一维自旋晶格模型中的结构与模式，该框架与统计力学在描述这些系统时是兼容的。

Abstract: This work formalizes the notions of structure and pattern for three distinct one-dimensional spin-lattice models (finite-range Ising, solid-on-solid, and three-body), using information-theoretic and computation-theoretic methods. We begin by presenting a novel derivation of the Boltzmann distribution for finite one-dimensional spin configurations embedded in infinite ones. We next recast this distribution as a stochastic process, thereby enabling us to analyze each spin-lattice model within the theory of computational mechanics. In this framework, the process's structure is quantified by excess entropy (predictable information) and statistical complexity (stored information), and the process's structure-generating mechanism is specified by its epsilon-machine. To assess compatibility with statistical mechanics, we compare the configurations jointly determined by the information measures and epsilon-machines to typical configurations drawn from the Boltzmann distribution, and we find agreement. We also include a self-contained primer on computational mechanics and provide code implementing the information measures and spin-model distributions.

</details>


### [118] [Wave Resistance for Stochastic Motion at Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08732)
*Maxence Arutkin,Shlomi Reuveni,Elie Raphael*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究随机轨迹下移动源在流体界面产生的平均波浪阻力，发现低于确定性辐射阈值时存在有限阻力，并正则化了毛细-重力波最小相速度处的奇异响应。推导了漂移布朗运动的标度律，并将波浪阻力理论推广至非高斯轨迹（Lévy飞行）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波浪阻力理论主要关注确定性运动轨迹，而对随机或随机轨迹下的平均阻力行为缺乏理解，尤其是低于辐射阈值时的有限阻力机制和奇异响应的正则化问题。

Method: 基于系综平均表面轮廓方法，从轨迹历史出发计算平均波浪阻力；分别对漂移布朗运动和漂移Lévy飞行进行理论推导，得到显式标度律和闭式解。

Result: 发现随机轨迹下存在低于确定性辐射阈值的有限阻力；毛细-重力波最小相速度处的奇异响应被正则化；漂移布朗运动表现出普适的高扩散衰减标度律；漂移Lévy飞行的平均波浪阻力得到闭式解。

Conclusion: 将波浪阻力理论从确定性轨迹扩展至随机轨迹（包括非高斯过程），揭示了随机性对阻力产生和奇异响应正则化的重要作用。

Abstract: Wave resistance is the drag generated by the wave radiation that a source moving at a fluid interface sustains. Under stochastic trajectories, the mean drag is controlled by the ensemble-averaged surface profile built from the trajectory history. We show that the result is a finite resistance below the deterministic radiation threshold and a regularization of the singular response at the minimum phase velocity of the capillary-gravity waves. We derive explicit scaling laws for drifted Brownian trajectories, including a universal high-diffusivity decay. For drifted Lévy flight, we find the mean wave resistance in closed-form, extending wave-drag theory to non-Gaussian trajectories.

</details>


### [119] [Fluctuation-stable generalized entropy probes of spectral heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08801)
*Arpita Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了广义熵诊断的涨落稳定性框架，从理论上解释了q>1区域物理鲁棒性的原因，并引入熵梯度磁化率χ_q作为粗粒化探针，通过Aubry-André模型验证了其区分均匀局域化转变与迁移率边共存相的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义熵测度中q>1区域经验上表现出更好的数值稳定性和干净的标度行为，但缺乏理论基础。本文旨在建立严格的涨落稳定性框架来解释这一现象。

Method: 发展了广义熵诊断的涨落稳定性框架；通过热力学标度分析识别渐近稳定临界阈值；引入熵梯度磁化率χ_q作为粗粒化探针；在Aubry-André和广义Aubry-André模型上验证。

Result: 证明了q<1时弱振幅谱涨落被放大，q>1时被抑制，提供了q>1物理鲁棒性的理论依据；χ_q能清晰区分均匀局域化转变与迁移率边共存相。

Conclusion: 涨落稳定性是准周期系统中广义熵诊断的指导原则。

Abstract: Generalized entropy measures are widely used to characterize localization and multifractality, and the regime \(q>1\) is often empirically found to exhibit improved numerical stability and cleaner scaling behavior. Here, we develop a fluctuation-stability framework for generalized entropy diagnostics and show that weak-amplitude spectral fluctuations are amplified for \(q<1\) and suppressed for \(q>1\), thereby providing a theoretical basis for the physically robust \(q>1\) regime. A thermodynamic scaling analysis further identifies an asymptotically stable regime beyond a critical threshold. As an application, we introduce the entropy-gradient susceptibility \(χ_q\) as a coarse-grained probe of spectral heterogeneity. Using the Aubry-André and generalized Aubry-André models, we demonstrate that \(χ_q\) sharply distinguishes homogeneous localization transitions from mobility-edge coexistence regimes. Our results establish fluctuation stability as a guiding principle for generalized entropy diagnostics in quasiperiodic systems.

</details>


### [120] [Time Evolution of Heat Conduction in a Generalized Model of Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08839)
*T. Koide,F. Nicacio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类包含动量和位置噪声-耗散项的广义谐振子网络热传导模型，该模型与GKSL方程一致。基于随机能量学推导了稳态热流解析表达式，验证了傅里叶定律和微观热边界阻力（类Kapitza电阻）。通过数值求解关联函数分析了热传导时间演化，发现广义模型产生连续但处处不可微的轨迹（过阻尼特征），且当粒子间相互作用开启时，热流出现由相互作用性质（吸引/排斥）严格决定的瞬时行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准布朗运动模型在热流定义（基于速度）上与GKSL量子主方程存在数学不一致性，因此需要构建一种更通用的随机热力学框架，以统一描述经典和量子非平衡热传导过程。

Method: 采用包含动量和位置双重噪声-耗散项的广义布朗运动模型（与GKSL方程相容），基于随机能量学框架推导稳态热流的解析表达式；通过数值求解关联函数的微分方程分析热传导的时间演化动力学。

Result: 1）稳态下傅里叶定律成立，模型自然产生微观热边界电阻；2）广义模型生成连续但无处可微的动量和位置轨迹（过阻尼特征）；3）相互作用开启时出现瞬时热流，方向由相互作用力性质（吸引/排斥）严格决定，与标准模型显著不同。

Conclusion: 该广义模型可作为模拟非平衡过程的有效现象学框架，为随机热力学与量子热力学的统一表述迈出关键一步。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of heat conduction in a network of harmonic oscillators interacting with heat baths, described by a generalized model of Brownian motion. This model includes noise and dissipation terms in both the momentum and position equations. This generalization is motivated by the requirement of consistency with the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) equation. Because standard definitions of heat current based on velocity become mathematically inconsistent in this framework, we derive an analytical expression for the steady-state heat flow based on an extended framework of stochastic energetics. We confirm that Fourier's law (linear thermal response) is satisfied and that the model naturally captures microscopic thermal boundary resistance, analogous to Kapitza resistance. This demonstrates that our generalized model functions as a valid phenomenological framework for simulating non-equilibrium processes, marking a crucial step toward a unified formulation of stochastic and quantum thermodynamics. Furthermore, we analyze the time evolution of heat conduction by numerically solving the corresponding differential equations for the correlation functions. Unlike standard Brownian motion, the generalized model generates continuous and nowhere differentiable trajectories for both momentum and position (as is characteristic of overdamped dynamics). Finally, we show that the heat current exhibits characteristic transient behavior when the inter-particle interaction is switched on. Specifically, an instantaneous heat flow emerges, whose direction is strictly governed by whether the interaction is attractive or repulsive, significantly differing from the predictions of the standard model.

</details>


### [121] [Free fermions in disguise without exponential degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09462)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个新的“伪装自由费米子”可解自旋链模型，其谱在一般耦合常数下不存在指数简并，打破了以往FFD模型普遍具有指数简并的模式。该模型可视为两个伊辛链的特定扰动，或标准Jordan-Wigner可解链与Fendley原始FFD模型之间的插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往发现的具有隐藏自由费米子结构的自旋链模型，其能谱都存在随体积指数增长的简并度，且对所有能级均匀分布。作者希望构造一个能摆脱这种指数简并的FFD模型，从而扩展可解模型的范围，并揭示隐藏自由费米子结构的更一般性质。

Method: 作者构造了一个特定的自旋链模型，可视为两个伊辛链的扰动，或作为标准Jordan-Wigner可解链与Fendley原始FFD模型之间的插值。通过隐藏自由费米子结构实现精确可解性，并分析其能谱简并性。文中还提及使用ChatGPT Pro作为研究助手协助部分工作。

Result: 得到了一个FFD可解模型，其谱在一般耦合常数下不存在指数简并，打破了此前所有FFD模型均匀指数简并的模式。

Conclusion: 成功构造了无指数简并的FFD可解模型，丰富了隐藏自由费米子可解模型家族，为理解可解模型中简并性的起源和结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recently, a number of spin chain models have been discovered that are solvable via hidden free-fermionic structures, going beyond the Jordan-Wigner paradigm. However, all examples in the literature displayed degeneracies that grow exponentially with the volume and that are homogeneous in the spectrum (identical degeneracies for all energy levels). In this note we present a model that can be solved by ``free fermions in disguise'' (FFD), such that the spectrum is free from exponential degeneracies for generic coupling constants. The model can be seen as a particular perturbation of two Ising chains. Alternatively, it can be realized as an interpolation between a standard Jordan-Wigner solvable chain and the original FFD model of Fendley. We used ChatGPT Pro 5.4 and 5.5 as a research assistant; in the Supplemental Material we provide details about the collaboration between the AI and the human author.

</details>


### [122] [Scaling Behaviors of Work Cumulants in Slow Isothermal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09552)
*Ruohan Xu,Yanbo Qiao,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究带能隙系统在慢等温过程中的功累积量，利用MSRDJ形式和关联函数性质，证明第n阶累积量按1/T^{n-1}标度，并从平衡态性质推导出系数，这些系数与热力学几何张量相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解慢等温过程中功的统计性质，揭示其标度行为与平衡态几何张量的联系。

Method: 采用Martin-Siggia-Rose-De Dominicis-Janssen (MSRDJ) 形式论以及连通关联函数的性质进行分析。

Result: 证明了慢等温过程中，第n阶功累积量标度为1/T^{n-1}；推导出累积量系数可由平衡态性质表达，且这些系数与热力学几何张量相关。

Conclusion: 揭示了慢等温过程中功累积量的普适标度律及其与平衡态几何张量的内在联系。

Abstract: We study the cumulants of work in a slow isothermal process for gapped systems. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose-De Dominicis-Janssen (MSRDJ) formalism and the properties of connected correlation functions, we show that in this process, the $n$-th cumulant of work scales as $1/T^{n-1}$ , where $T$ is the time duration. This result holds generally for arbitrary smooth protocols. Furthermore, we derive the coefficients of the cumulants from equilibrium properties. These coefficients are found to be relevant to thermodynamic geometric tensors.

</details>


### [123] [Geometric Dissipation Constraints in Stochastic Reaction Dynamics: A Variational Observable for Hidden Kinetic Structure in Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09684)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，用于刻画随机反应动力学中的隐藏动力学约束。通过从Wasserstein空间的梯度流出发，推导出一个变分泛函，其主导渐近结构定义了一个局部耗散-几何耦合可观测量，该量结合了力-漂移对齐和相空间压缩，能够区分在传统自由能分析下简并的动力学通道。在Muller-Brown势、周期性势场以及丙氨酸二肽构象转变等基准系统中验证了该方法的有效性，并观察到高频均匀化区域的二次缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由能垒和熵产生等全局热力学描述符对控制路径选择的局域几何结构不敏感，无法区分动力学位点简并的反应通道，因此需要新的几何框架揭示隐藏的动力学约束。

Method: 基于过阻尼Langevin动力学在Wasserstein空间中的梯度流表述，推导变分泛函；由其主导渐近结构构造局部耗散-几何耦合可观测量，结合力-漂移对齐与漂移场散度诱导的相空间压缩，得到反映能量景观二阶几何特征的标量场。

Result: 在Muller-Brown势、波纹周期性势场和丙氨酸二肽构象转变等基准系统中，该方法成功区分了传统自由能分析下简并的动力学通道，并表现出高频均匀化区域内的二次缩放行为。

Conclusion: 随机反应动力学中存在超出标准热力学或反应坐标描述的附加几何层动力学控制，该几何框架为理解分子运动的动力学约束提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a geometric framework for characterizing hidden kinetic constraints in stochastic reaction dynamics. While free-energy barriers and entropy production provide global descriptors of thermodynamic behavior, they are largely insensitive to local geometric structure in configuration space that governs pathway selection. Starting from overdamped Langevin dynamics formulated as a gradient flow in Wasserstein space, we derive a variational functional whose leading-order asymptotic structure defines a local dissipation-geometry coupling observable. This quantity combines force-drift alignment with phase-space contraction induced by the divergence of the drift field, yielding a scalar field that reflects second-order geometric features of the underlying energy landscape. We demonstrate that this observable distinguishes kinetically distinct reaction channels that are degenerate under conventional free-energy analysis, as shown through numerical experiments on benchmark systems including the Muller-Brown potential, corrugated periodic landscapes, and the conformational transitions of Alanine Dipeptide. These experiments demonstrate robust separation of pathways and are consistent with a quadratic scaling behavior in the high-frequency homogenization regime. Our results suggest that stochastic reaction dynamics contain an additional geometric layer of kinetic control in molecular motion not captured by standard thermodynamic or reaction-coordinate descriptions.

</details>
